     NOTES:
     1. The netname "VCC_1P8V" represents connection to the +1.8V digital power plane.                                                                                                                           COMPUTER GENERATED DRAWING. DO NOT REVISE MANUALLY
     2. The netname "VCC_2P5V" represents connection to the +2.5V digital power plane.                                                                                                                                                               REVISION
     3. The netname "VCC_3P3V" represents connection to the +3.3V digital power plane.                                                                                                                REV                      DESCRIPTION                          DATE           APPROVED
     4. The netname "GND" represents connection to the ground plane.                                                                                                                                  A       Initial Release: baselined from 2510299 Rev D.       08/17/08
     5. A "#" suffix on a signal plane indicates an active low signal.
     6. All components with designators "U", "Q", and "D" are electrostatic discharge sensitive.                                                                                                          B   See Revisions Section for Details                    03/23/23
     7. All resistor values are in ohms unless otherwise specified.
                                                                                                                                                                                                          C   See Revisions Section for Details                    06/01/23
     8. All capacitor values in microfarads unless otherwise specified.
     9. Bluewire cicuit modifications are noted by dashed outlines.
